      "cell_type": "markdown",
      "source": [
        "# Athlete Physiological Performance Analysis and VO₂ Prediction\n",
        "\n",
        "## Project Overview\n",
        "\n",
        "This project investigates the use of physiological, demographic, and\n",
        "exercise-related data to estimate oxygen consumption (VO₂) during\n",
        "maximal treadmill exercise.\n",
        "\n",
        "The project is inspired by existing research on machine learning-based\n",
        "VO₂ estimation using the University of Málaga Treadmill Maximal Exercise\n",
        "Tests dataset available through PhysioNet.\n",
        "\n",
        "The project consists of two main stages:\n",
        "\n",
        "1. Reproducing and evaluating a machine learning-based VO₂ prediction\n",
        "   pipeline using physiological exercise data.\n",
        "\n",
        "2. Extending the analysis through exploratory data analysis, correlation\n",
        "   analysis, feature importance, physiological response analysis, and\n",
        "   group-wise evaluation.\n",
        "\n",
        "The overall goal is not only to develop an accurate prediction model but\n",
        "also to investigate the physiological factors and relationships associated\n",
        "with oxygen consumption during exercise."
      ],
      "metadata": {
        "id": "0wjQiFZZZjsq"
      }
    },
    {
      "cell_type": "markdown",

              "        const element = document.querySelector('#df-259094f0-8f33-4772-ba52-dd96f06597c6');\n",
              "        const dataTable =\n",
              "          await google.colab.kernel.invokeFunction('convertToInteractive',\n",
              "                                                    [key], {});\n",
              "        if (!dataTable) return;\n",
              "\n",
              "        const docLinkHtml = 'Like what you see? Visit the ' +\n",
              "          '<a target=\"_blank\" href=https://colab.research.google.com/notebooks/data_table.ipynb>data table notebook</a>'\n",
              "          + ' to learn more about interactive tables.';\n",
              "        element.innerHTML = '';\n",
              "        dataTable['output_type'] = 'display_data';\n",
              "        await google.colab.output.renderOutput(dataTable, element);\n",
              "        const docLink = document.createElement('div');\n",
              "        docLink.innerHTML = docLinkHtml;\n",
              "        element.appendChild(docLink);\n",
              "      }\n",
              "    </script>\n",
              "  </div>\n",
              "\n",
              "\n",
              "    </div>\n",
              "  </div>\n"
            ],

        "## 12. Modelling Setup\n",
        "\n",
        "A subject-level split is used so that the test set contains unseen subjects."
      ],
      "metadata": {
        "id": "ncWYxETEja2S"
      }
    },
    {
      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "# Define baseline features\n",
        "baseline_features = [\n",
        "    \"time\",\n",
        "    \"Speed\",\n",
        "    \"HR\",\n",
        "    \"Age\",\n",
        "    \"Weight\",\n",
        "    \"Height\",\n",
        "    \"Humidity\",\n",
        "    \"Temperature\",\n",
        "    \"Sex\"\n",
        "]\n",
        "\n",
        "# Define prediction target\n",
        "target = \"VO2\"\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"Baseline features:\")\n",
        "print(baseline_features)\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"\\nTarget:\")\n",
        "print(target)"
      ],
      "metadata": {
        "colab": {
          "base_uri": "https://localhost:8080/"

    {
      "cell_type": "markdown",
      "source": [
        "Perform the Subject-Level Split"
      ],
      "metadata": {
        "id": "VkZYbOQYjeDQ"
      }
    },
    {
      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "from sklearn.model_selection import GroupShuffleSplit\n",
        "\n",
        "# Create the group-based splitter\n",
        "group_split = GroupShuffleSplit(\n",
        "    n_splits=1,\n",
        "    test_size=0.20,\n",
        "    random_state=42\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "# Generate train and test indices based on subject IDs\n",
        "train_idx, test_idx = next(\n",
        "    group_split.split(\n",
        "        model_df,\n",
        "        groups=model_df[\"ID_subject\"]\n",
        "    )\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "# Create training and testing datasets\n",
        "train_df = model_df.iloc[train_idx].copy()\n",
        "test_df_model = model_df.iloc[test_idx].copy()\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"Training data shape:\", train_df.shape)\n",
        "print(\"Testing data shape:\", test_df_model.shape)\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"\\nTraining subjects:\", train_df[\"ID_subject\"].nunique())\n",
        "print(\"Testing subjects:\", test_df_model[\"ID_subject\"].nunique())\n",
        "\n",
        "# Verify that subjects do not overlap\n",
        "overlap = set(train_df[\"ID_subject\"]).intersection(\n",
        "    set(test_df_model[\"ID_subject\"])\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"\\nOverlapping subjects:\", len(overlap))"
      ],
      "metadata": {
        "colab": {
          "base_uri": "https://localhost:8080/"
        },
        "id": "tXvEZF6qjcHx",
        "outputId": "a5f3a01f-07b7-4d51-ff48-21c6139530cb"

      "cell_type": "markdown",
      "source": [
        "## 13. Feature Preprocessing\n",
        "\n",
        "Missing numerical values are handled using median imputation fitted on the training data."
      ],
      "metadata": {
        "id": "AkB7VMpWjm4X"
      }
    },
    {
      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "# Separate features and target\n",
        "\n",
        "X_train = train_df[baseline_features].copy()\n",
        "X_test = test_df_model[baseline_features].copy()\n",
        "\n",
        "y_train = train_df[target].copy()\n",
        "y_test = test_df_model[target].copy()\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"X_train shape:\", X_train.shape)\n",
        "print(\"X_test shape:\", X_test.shape)\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"y_train shape:\", y_train.shape)\n",
        "print(\"y_test shape:\", y_test.shape)"
      ],
      "metadata": {
        "colab": {

    {
      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "from sklearn.impute import SimpleImputer\n",
        "\n",
        "# Create median imputer\n",
        "imputer = SimpleImputer(strategy=\"median\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# Fit only on training data and transform both datasets\n",
        "X_train_imputed = imputer.fit_transform(X_train)\n",
        "X_test_imputed = imputer.transform(X_test)\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"Missing values in X_train after imputation:\",\n",
        "      pd.DataFrame(X_train_imputed).isnull().sum().sum())\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"Missing values in X_test after imputation:\",\n",
        "      pd.DataFrame(X_test_imputed).isnull().sum().sum())"
      ],
      "metadata": {
        "colab": {
          "base_uri": "https://localhost:8080/"
        },

        "outputId": "df376b7a-3a7e-436b-f37d-90e4349ae45f"
      },
      "execution_count": null,
      "outputs": [
        {
          "output_type": "stream",
          "name": "stdout",

            "Missing values in X_test after imputation: 0\n"

    {
      "cell_type": "markdown",
      "source": [
        "## 14. Baseline Model\n",
        "\n",
        "A linear regression model is used as a simple baseline for comparison."
      ],
      "metadata": {
        "id": "n9xlXPw6j0Ur"
      }
    },
    {
      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "from sklearn.linear_model import LinearRegression\n",
        "from sklearn.metrics import mean_absolute_error, mean_squared_error, r2_score\n",
        "import numpy as np\n",
        "\n",
        "# Train baseline Linear Regression model\n",
        "linear_model = LinearRegression()\n",
        "\n",
        "linear_model.fit(X_train_imputed, y_train)\n",
        "\n",
        "# Predictions\n",
        "y_pred_linear = linear_model.predict(X_test_imputed)\n",
        "\n",
        "# Evaluation\n",
        "mae_linear = mean_absolute_error(y_test, y_pred_linear)\n",
        "rmse_linear = np.sqrt(\n",
        "    mean_squared_error(y_test, y_pred_linear)\n",
        ")\n",
        "r2_linear = r2_score(y_test, y_pred_linear)\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"Linear Regression Results\")\n",
        "print(\"-------------------------\")\n",
        "print(f\"MAE:  {mae_linear:.2f}\")\n",
        "print(f\"RMSE: {rmse_linear:.2f}\")\n",
        "print(f\"R²:   {r2_linear:.4f}\")"
      ],
      "metadata": {
        "colab": {
          "base_uri": "https://localhost:8080/"
        },
        "id": "B7yqrNFbjrjC",

        "## 15. XGBoost Model\n",
        "\n",
        "An XGBoost regression model is trained to capture nonlinear relationships between physiological and demographic features."
      ],
      "metadata": {
        "id": "6ejyaCwhj4KP"
      }
    },
    {
      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "from xgboost import XGBRegressor\n",
        "\n",
        "# Create the XGBoost model\n",
        "xgb_model = XGBRegressor(\n",
        "    n_estimators=300,\n",
        "    max_depth=8,\n",
        "    learning_rate=0.05,\n",
        "    subsample=0.8,\n",
        "    colsample_bytree=0.8,\n",
        "    objective=\"reg:squarederror\",\n",
        "    random_state=42,\n",
        "    n_jobs=-1\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "# Train the model\n",
        "xgb_model.fit(\n",
        "    X_train_imputed,\n",
        "    y_train\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"XGBoost training completed.\")"
      ],
      "metadata": {
        "colab": {
          "base_uri": "https://localhost:8080/"

        "y_pred_xgb = xgb_model.predict(X_test_imputed)\n",
        "\n",
        "# Evaluation metrics\n",
        "mae_xgb = mean_absolute_error(y_test, y_pred_xgb)\n",
        "\n",
        "rmse_xgb = np.sqrt(\n",
        "    mean_squared_error(y_test, y_pred_xgb)\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "r2_xgb = r2_score(y_test, y_pred_xgb)\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"XGBoost Results\")\n",
        "print(\"----------------\")\n",
        "print(f\"MAE:  {mae_xgb:.2f}\")\n",

        "# Create a separate imputer for the extended feature set\n",
        "\n",
        "extended_imputer = SimpleImputer(strategy=\"median\")\n",
        "\n",
        "X_train_extended_imputed = extended_imputer.fit_transform(\n",
        "    X_train_extended\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "X_test_extended_imputed = extended_imputer.transform(\n",
        "    X_test_extended\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\n",
        "    \"Missing values after preprocessing:\",\n",
        "    pd.DataFrame(X_train_extended_imputed).isnull().sum().sum()\n",

      "cell_type": "markdown",
      "source": [
        "## 19. Extended Model Training\n",
        "\n",
        "The extended feature set is used to train an XGBoost model with the same configuration as the baseline model."
      ],
      "metadata": {
        "id": "wCfvauxNldFx"
      }
    },
    {
      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "# Create the extended XGBoost model\n",
        "\n",
        "xgb_extended_model = XGBRegressor(\n",
        "    n_estimators=300,\n",
        "    max_depth=8,\n",
        "    learning_rate=0.05,\n",
        "    subsample=0.8,\n",
        "    colsample_bytree=0.8,\n",
        "    objective=\"reg:squarederror\",\n",
        "    random_state=42,\n",
        "    n_jobs=-1\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "# Train the model\n",
        "xgb_extended_model.fit(\n",
        "    X_train_extended_imputed,\n",
        "    y_train\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"Extended XGBoost training completed.\")"
      ],
      "metadata": {
        "colab": {
          "base_uri": "https://localhost:8080/"
        },
        "id": "kZqeWj-9lPzM",
        "outputId": "a819d597-85f9-4f01-edba-9f75ac11aa84"
      },
      "execution_count": null,
      "outputs": [
        {
          "output_type": "stream",
          "name": "stdout",
          "text": [
            "Extended XGBoost training completed.\n"
          ]
        }
      ]
    },
    {
      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "# Predictions using the extended model\n",
        "y_pred_extended = xgb_extended_model.predict(\n",
        "    X_test_extended_imputed\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "# Evaluation\n",
        "mae_extended = mean_absolute_error(\n",
        "    y_test,\n",
        "    y_pred_extended\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "rmse_extended = np.sqrt(\n",
        "    mean_squared_error(\n",
        "        y_test,\n",
        "        y_pred_extended\n",
        "    )\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "r2_extended = r2_score(\n",
        "    y_test,\n",
        "    y_pred_extended\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"Extended XGBoost Results\")\n",
        "print(\"------------------------\")\n",
        "print(f\"MAE:  {mae_extended:.2f}\")\n",
        "print(f\"RMSE: {rmse_extended:.2f}\")\n",
        "print(f\"R²:   {r2_extended:.4f}\")"

        "The extended XGBoost model achieved the strongest performance with an R² score of approximately 0.945. Adding respiratory variables—particularly VCO₂ and VE—substantially improved VO₂ prediction compared with the baseline model based primarily on demographic and exercise variables."

        "## 24. Saving Trained Models and Results"
      ],
      "metadata": {
        "id": "tsDCk6cqnYKC"
      }

    {
      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "import os\n",
        "import joblib\n",
        "\n",
        "# Create a folder for saved project files\n",
        "os.makedirs(\"saved_models\", exist_ok=True)\n",
        "\n",
        "# Save XGBoost models\n",
        "xgb_model.save_model(\"saved_models/baseline_xgboost.json\")\n",
        "xgb_extended_model.save_model(\"saved_models/extended_xgboost.json\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# Save imputers\n",
        "joblib.dump(imputer, \"saved_models/baseline_imputer.pkl\")\n",
        "joblib.dump(extended_imputer, \"saved_models/extended_imputer.pkl\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# Save feature lists\n",
        "joblib.dump(baseline_features, \"saved_models/baseline_features.pkl\")\n",
        "joblib.dump(extended_features, \"saved_models/extended_features.pkl\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# Save final results\n",
        "final_results.to_csv(\n",
        "    \"saved_models/model_results.csv\",\n",
        "    index=False\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"Project files saved successfully!\")\n",
        "print(\"\\nSaved files:\")\n",
        "print(os.listdir(\"saved_models\"))"

              "    async function download(id, filename, size) {\n",
              "      if (!google.colab.kernel.accessAllowed) {\n",
              "        return;\n",
              "      }\n",
              "      const div = document.createElement('div');\n",
              "      const label = document.createElement('label');\n",
              "      label.textContent = `Downloading \"${filename}\": `;\n",
              "      div.appendChild(label);\n",
              "      const progress = document.createElement('progress');\n",
              "      progress.max = size;\n",
              "      div.appendChild(progress);\n",
              "      document.body.appendChild(div);\n",
              "\n",
              "      const buffers = [];\n",
              "      let downloaded = 0;\n",
              "\n",
              "      const channel = await google.colab.kernel.comms.open(id);\n",
              "      // Send a message to notify the kernel that we're ready.\n",
              "      channel.send({})\n",
              "\n",
              "      for await (const message of channel.messages) {\n",
              "        // Send a message to notify the kernel that we're ready.\n",
              "        channel.send({})\n",
              "        if (message.buffers) {\n",
              "          for (const buffer of message.buffers) {\n",
              "            buffers.push(buffer);\n",
              "            downloaded += buffer.byteLength;\n",
              "            progress.value = downloaded;\n",
              "          }\n",
              "        }\n",
              "      }\n",
              "      const blob = new Blob(buffers, {type: 'application/binary'});\n",
              "      const a = document.createElement('a');\n",
              "      a.href = window.URL.createObjectURL(blob);\n",
              "      a.download = filename;\n",
              "      div.appendChild(a);\n",
              "      a.click();\n",
              "      div.remove();\n",
              "    }\n",
              "  "
            ]

        "## 25. VO₂ Prediction Demonstration"
      ],
      "metadata": {
        "id": "rT3RznZwnkE3"

    },
    {
      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "def predict_vo2(\n",
        "    time,\n",
        "    speed,\n",
        "    hr,\n",
        "    age,\n",
        "    weight,\n",
        "    height,\n",
        "    humidity,\n",
        "    temperature,\n",
        "    sex,\n",
        "    vco2,\n",
        "    rr,\n",
        "    ve\n",
        "):\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Create input data in the same feature order\n",
        "    input_data = pd.DataFrame(\n",
        "        [[\n",
        "            time,\n",
        "            speed,\n",
        "            hr,\n",
        "            age,\n",
        "            weight,\n",
        "            height,\n",
        "            humidity,\n",
        "            temperature,\n",
        "            sex,\n",
        "            vco2,\n",
        "            rr,\n",
        "            ve\n",
        "        ]],\n",
        "        columns=extended_features\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Apply preprocessing\n",
        "    input_imputed = extended_imputer.transform(\n",
        "        input_data\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Predict VO2\n",
        "    prediction = xgb_extended_model.predict(\n",
        "        input_imputed\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    return prediction[0]"
      ],
      "metadata": {
        "id": "PCHXIrScnfq1"
      },

        "## 26. Model Interpretability with SHAP and LIME\n",
        "\n",
        "In this section, we apply **SHAP (SHapley Additive exPlanations)** and **LIME (Local Interpretable Model-agnostic Explanations)** to explain the predictions of our `xgb_extended_model`."
      ]
    },
    {
      "cell_type": "code",
      "metadata": {
        "id": "f4fc2e50"
      },
      "source": [
        "!pip install -q shap lime"
      ],
      "execution_count": null,
      "outputs": []
    },
    {
      "cell_type": "markdown",
      "metadata": {
        "id": "956e4494"
      },
      "source": [
        "### SHAP Analysis\n",
        "\n",
        "SHAP values attribute the prediction of each feature to the difference between the actual prediction and the average prediction. We will use a background summary of our training data and visualize the global impact of features, followed by a local prediction explanation."
      ]
    },
    {
      "cell_type": "code",
      "metadata": {
        "colab": {
          "base_uri": "https://localhost:8080/",
          "height": 637
        },
        "id": "fb4a352b",
        "outputId": "6e286f1a-c40d-4f24-8556-bd0fb0a2e39e"
      },
      "source": [
        "import shap\n",
        "import joblib\n",
        "import pandas as pd\n",
        "import numpy as np\n",
        "import matplotlib.pyplot as plt\n",
        "import os\n",
        "from xgboost import XGBRegressor\n",
        "\n",
        "# Ensure we have our model and variables loaded\n",
        "try:\n",
        "    # Try to load existing variables from the saved directory\n",
        "    if not os.path.exists('saved_models'):\n",
        "        print(\"Rebuilding dataset and preprocessing parameters as saved_models directory does not exist...\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Load feature lists\n",
        "    extended_features = joblib.load(\"saved_models/extended_features.pkl\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Load model\n",
        "    xgb_extended_model = XGBRegressor()\n",
        "    xgb_extended_model.load_model(\"saved_models/extended_xgboost.json\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Re-import or ensure X_train_extended_imputed and other objects exist in memory\n",
        "    if 'X_train_extended_imputed' not in globals():\n",
        "        subject_df = pd.read_csv(\"subject-info.csv\")\n",
        "        test_df = pd.read_csv(\"test_measure.csv\")\n",
        "        merged_df = test_df.merge(subject_df, on=\"ID_test\", how=\"inner\", suffixes=('_measurement', '_subject')).drop_duplicates()\n",
        "        model_df = merged_df[merged_df[\"VO2\"] > 0].copy()\n",
        "        model_df.loc[model_df[\"HR\"] < 30, \"HR\"] = np.nan\n",
        "\n",
        "        from sklearn.model_selection import GroupShuffleSplit\n",
        "        group_split = GroupShuffleSplit(n_splits=1, test_size=0.20, random_state=42)\n",
        "        train_idx, test_idx = next(group_split.split(model_df, groups=model_df[\"ID_subject\"]))\n",
        "        train_df = model_df.iloc[train_idx].copy()\n",
        "        test_df_model = model_df.iloc[test_idx].copy()\n",
        "\n",
        "        X_train_extended = train_df[extended_features].copy()\n",
        "        X_test_extended = test_df_model[extended_features].copy()\n",
        "        y_test = test_df_model[\"VO2\"].copy()\n",
        "\n",
        "        from sklearn.impute import SimpleImputer\n",
        "        extended_imputer = joblib.load(\"saved_models/extended_imputer.pkl\")\n",
        "        X_train_extended_imputed = extended_imputer.transform(X_train_extended)\n",
        "        X_test_extended_imputed = extended_imputer.transform(X_test_extended)\n",
        "except Exception as e:\n",
        "    print(f\"Could not load saved models automatically: {e}. Please ensure previous cells have been executed first.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# --- SWITCH TO TREESHAP ---\n",
        "# Create the TreeExplainer optimized specifically for tree ensembles like XGBoost\n",
        "explainer = shap.TreeExplainer(xgb_extended_model)\n",
        "\n",
        "# Calculate exact SHAP values for the test sample\n",
        "# We will sample 1000 observations from the test set for a richer global summary (runs instantly with TreeSHAP!)\n",
        "np.random.seed(42)\n",
        "sample_indices = np.random.choice(len(X_test_extended_imputed), 1000, replace=False)\n",
        "X_test_sample = X_test_extended_imputed[sample_indices]\n",
        "\n",
        "# Compute exact SHAP values\n",
        "shap_values = explainer.shap_values(X_test_sample)\n",
        "\n",
        "# Global feature summary plot\n",
        "plt.figure(figsize=(10, 6))\n",
        "shap.summary_plot(shap_values, X_test_sample, feature_names=extended_features, show=False)\n",
        "plt.title(\"Exact SHAP Summary Plot via TreeSHAP (Extended Model)\", fontsize=14)\n",
        "plt.tight_layout()\n",
        "plt.show()"
      ],
      "execution_count": null,

        "#### Interpretation of SHAP Global Insights:\n",
        "- **Carbon Dioxide Production ($VCO_2$)**: This is the strongest overall driver. High $VCO_2$ production (shown in pink) has a massive positive impact on predicted oxygen consumption, reflecting the tight physiological coupling between ventilation, metabolic demands, and oxygen extraction.\n",
        "- **Speed & Minute Ventilation ($VE$)**: High treadmill speeds and elevated volume of expired air ($VE$) consistently shift the model's $VO_2$ predictions upwards.\n",
        "- **Demographics & Environment**: Younger age profiles and being male are associated with positive shifts in maximum oxygen consumption, while variables like relative humidity and respiratory rate ($RR$) demonstrate low overall global impact."

        "### LIME Analysis\n",
        "\n",
        "LIME builds local surrogate models around individual predictions to explain how the model behaves locally."
      ]
    },
    {
      "cell_type": "code",
      "metadata": {
        "colab": {
          "base_uri": "https://localhost:8080/",
          "height": 539

        "id": "54cf374d",
        "outputId": "72a3ef6c-f9cf-43c3-cfdb-1206a425fc32"

      "source": [
        "from lime import lime_tabular\n",
        "\n",
        "# Initialize the LIME Explainer on the training data\n",
        "lime_explainer = lime_tabular.LimeTabularExplainer(\n",
        "    training_data=X_train_extended_imputed,\n",
        "    feature_names=extended_features,\n",
        "    class_names=[\"VO2\"],\n",
        "    mode=\"regression\",\n",
        "    random_state=42\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "# Select a single test instance to explain (e.g., the first instance in our sample)\n",
        "single_instance = X_test_sample[0]\n",
        "actual_vo2 = y_test.iloc[sample_indices[0]]\n",
        "predicted_vo2 = xgb_extended_model.predict(single_instance.reshape(1, -1))[0]\n",
        "\n",
        "print(f\"Explaining prediction for sample index {sample_indices[0]}:\")\n",
        "print(f\"Actual VO2: {actual_vo2:.2f}\")\n",
        "print(f\"Predicted VO2: {predicted_vo2:.2f}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# Explain the instance prediction\n",
        "exp = lime_explainer.explain_instance(\n",
        "    data_row=single_instance,\n",
        "    predict_fn=xgb_extended_model.predict,\n",
        "    num_features=6\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "# Plot the local explanation\n",
        "fig = exp.as_pyplot_figure()\n",
        "plt.title(\"LIME Local Explanation for VO2 Prediction\", fontsize=14)\n",
        "plt.tight_layout()\n",
        "plt.show()"

        "### Custom Local Explainer Interface\n",
        "\n",
        "We can create a wrapper function that accepts specific athlete feature values (either as a dictionary or a structured array), preprocesses them, and plots a local LIME explanation to see which factors drive their predicted $VO_2$."
      ]
    },
    {
      "cell_type": "code",
      "metadata": {
        "colab": {
          "base_uri": "https://localhost:8080/",
          "height": 522
        },
        "id": "68944734",
        "outputId": "f8ceef31-9303-4338-d8b2-9af66da32dab"
      },
      "source": [
        "def explain_custom_athlete(feature_dict):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Generates a local LIME explanation for a custom dictionary of features.\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    # 1. Convert input dictionary to DataFrame with correct feature order\n",
        "    custom_df = pd.DataFrame([feature_dict], columns=extended_features)\n",
        "\n",
        "    # 2. Preprocess / Impute missing values using the trained extended imputer\n",
        "    custom_imputed = extended_imputer.transform(custom_df)[0]\n",
        "\n",
        "    # 3. Predict VO2\n",
        "    prediction = xgb_extended_model.predict(custom_imputed.reshape(1, -1))[0]\n",
        "    print(f\"Predicted VO2 for this specific profile: {prediction:.2f} mL/min\\n\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    # 4. Generate local LIME explanation\n",
        "    explanation = lime_explainer.explain_instance(\n",
        "        data_row=custom_imputed,\n",
